Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biochemistry variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 342 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 304 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_342(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 342. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^304(S, F) is trivial. QED.
